The full original coefficients, signs, flow, exact expenses and actual screens follow.\n\n# Three explicit nonlocal terminal transports and their original-space failures\n\nJOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT — UNVERIFIED. Worker max, invocation75 after\nassigned ended baseline74, ended ceiling75, attempted ceiling76, cap1.\nThis manuscript is shared research evidence, not a fact. No formal gate,\nmathematical program, arithmetic replay, executed trajectory/recurrence,\noptimizer, installation, consult or subagent was used. Actual controller\ncompletion and counters are not observed here.\n\nThe three laws change the terminal outgoing probability on EVERY original\nfinite prefix query, and add a completely specified coupled remote prefix\nflow. They are constructed arrays, not selections from an unknown feasible\nset. The uniform and physical-mass laws fail an old innovation direction.\nThe subsidized law passes the ENTIRE old two-dimensional innovation plane\nand the actual root bridge/right-first gradient plane, but fails a larger\noriginal dyadic innovation. Each failure is at the same physical enclosing\nm, at every real objective level q. None is a W bound or a primal witness.\n\n## 1. Target, actual word and accepted inputs\n\nFor a nonnegative word h of length n, W_n(h) is the supremum of\n2(f(x_n)-f(x_star))/(L||x_0-x_star||^2), over all finite-dimensional\nEuclidean spaces, convex differentiable functions with L-Lipschitz\ngradient and a minimizer x_star, L>0, x_0!=x_star, under\nx_(i+1)=x_i-(h_i/L)grad f(x_i). This is W=2R.\nThe original benchmark, with every integer split retained, is\n\n    C_0=B_0=1,\n    Omega(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt((x+y)^2+4xy))/2,\n    G(x,y)=2x+y/2+sqrt(y^2+8xy)/2,\n    C_n=max_(0<=k<n)Omega(C_k,C_(n-k-1)),\n    B_n=max_(0<=k<n)G(C_k,B_(n-k-1)), U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.\n\nFix ONE enclosing integer m>=1536, and throughout put\n\n    lambda=1+sqrt(2), epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon,\n    c=lambda(1+epsilon), a=beta/c,\n    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j),\n    n_j=2^j-1, S_j=sum H_j, A_j=1+S_j,\n    d_j=a*c^j, D_j=2A_j-1, t_j=d_j/A_j, t_star=1/lambda.\n\nAll children use these SAME enclosing parameters. The actual diagnostic\nword is h=H_m=H_(m,1/4); its physical entries are not altered or reordered.\nEpsilon=0 will be a Silver calibration only. An identity on child H_j\nalways uses the original enclosing m, never epsilon=1/(4j).\n\nAccepted inputs actually read are fact1252b8823bb5a290 in full (original\ninterpolation, every formal vector, and exact terminal-square theorem),\nfact04219554e41d9750 Sections10–11 (primitive and objective conventions),\nand fact5fd396eba75651e7 Section10 for ONLY\n\n    B_(n_m)/D_m<237/250, m>=1536.\n\nA full all-function upper W_(n_m)(H_m)<=zeta_m/D_m with\nsup_m zeta_m<250/237 would therefore give a fixed improvement on\nunbounded horizons. The restricted-family optimum in the last accepted\nfact supplies no such upper. No original target is established here.\n\nThe full shared unverified1e4b5e4fdcb5a60f and f10a0fd97ba2b881,\nincluding the scoped failures f251f43c220abd77,57162242c42d77eb,\n309b078dc5f686e6, were read. They are awareness, not predecessors.\nEvery new formula reused from that regime is derived in THIS joint draft.\nGuidancec8160224c76ffea9 and elaborationd53d7eabce096c9c govern this round.\n\n## 2. Original full matrix and exact expenditure, jointly derived\n\nNormalize smoothness1 and x_star=g_star=f_star=0. Retain the independent\noriginal formal vectors (x_0,g_0,...,g_n), including terminal, bridge,\nand any zero-step coordinates. Put\n\n    x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t, Ppos_i=sum_(t<i)h_t,\n    S=sum h, D=1+2S, h_n=0,\n    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.\n\nThe residual convention is exactly fact1252b8823bb5a290; every actual\nlegal transcript has Q_ij<=0. Let K_ij be the finite multipliers,\nnu_i the i->star rows, xiCert_i the star->i rows, and K_ii=0.\nValue cancellation is precisely\n\n    sum_j K_ij-sum_j K_ji+nu_i-xiCert_i=2*1_(i=n).       (1)\n\nAfter it, the entire certificate is\n\n    M(q)=q||x_0||^2-2f_n+sum K_ij Q_ij\n                  +sum nu_i Q_(i,star)+sum xiCert_i Q_(star,i)\n        =q||x_0||^2-sum nu_i<x_0,g_i>+Cgrad(g).        (2)\n\nWith out_i=nu_i+sum_j K_ij, collecting each diagonal and each ordered\nposition term yields\n\n    Cgrad_ii=out_i-1_(i=n)-h_i sum_(v>i)K_iv,\n    2Cgrad_ij=-(K_ij+K_ji)-h_j sum_(v>j)K_iv\n                           +h_i[nu_j+sum_(v<=i)K_jv], i<j. (3)\n\nIndeed half the full incident mass at i is out_i-1_(i=n) by (1).\nA forward position term contributes -h_i K_iv to its source diagonal.\nFor i<j the gradient-difference term is -(K_ij+K_ji); source i's\nlater destinations contribute -h_j sum_(v>j)K_iv, and all source j\ndestinations at or before i, together with its outgoing optimum row,\ncontribute the last term. This proves the complete matrix, including\nthe initial mixed row -nu/2 and all original off-diagonals.\nFor q>0, full PSD is equivalent to\n\n    Cgrad-nu nu^T/(4q)>=0                              (4)\n\non the ENTIRE gradient space. A selected plane does not replace (4).\n\nFor completeness both optimum directions are priced before specializing\nto the three laws. Let e be the all-ones gradient column, csum=Cgrad e,\nX=sum xiCert_i, E=sum(1+Ppos_i)xiCert_i, and Hprice=D X-E.\nSumming (1) gives sum nu_i=2+X. Multiplying it by Ppos_i and summing\ngives sum K_ij(Ppos_i-Ppos_j)=2S-sum(nu_i-xiCert_i)Ppos_i.\nExpansion at a common gradient then gives\n\n    e^T Cgrad e=D+E,\n    M(x_0=r,g=t e)=q r^2-(2+X)r t+(D+E)t^2.           (5)\n\nThe one-sided Huber function, zero for z<=0, z^2/2 on[0,1] and\nz-1/2 above1, is convex and1-smooth. At x_0=D all samples lie above1:\ng_i=1, x_i=D-Ppos_i, f_i=D-Ppos_i-1/2. Finite and outgoing optimum\nresiduals vanish; Q_(star,i)=-(2S-Ppos_i). At q=zeta/D this gives\n\n    M(D,e)=(zeta-1)D-Hprice.                           (6)\n\nWhole-word necessary prices are Hprice<=(zeta-1)D and\nzeta>=D(2+X)^2/[4(D+E)]. These are single total allowances.\nCompleting the common-gradient/initial-point block, with v in e-perp,\ngives its exact transverse charge. If\n\n    DeltaCommon=q(D+E)-(2+X)^2/4>0,\n\nthe charge is\n\n    Price(v)=[(D+E)(nu^T v)^2/4\n       -(2+X)(nu^T v)(csum^T v)/2+q(csum^T v)^2]/DeltaCommon. (7)\n\nThis follows by inverting [[q,-(2+X)/2],[-(2+X)/2,D+E]] and\nusing the coupling (-nu^T v/2,csum^T v). In the singular case the\ncoupling must annihilate that block's kernel; no mode is discarded.\n\nOur constructed laws have xiCert=0. They therefore have sum nu=2,\ne^T Cgrad e=D, Hprice=0 and exact Huber slack(zeta-1)D. Set\ndcontact=csum-D nu/2, so e^T dcontact=0. Equation(7) becomes\n\n    Price(v)=(csum^T v)^2/D+(dcontact^T v)^2/[D(zeta-1)]. (8)\n\nAt the advertised zeta_0=101/100 the second term has coefficient100/D.\nEquations(2)–(8) retain EVERY initial, common and transverse direction.\nThey are calculations in this joint UNVERIFIED draft, not accepted bricks.\nAny negative direction at x_0=0 survives every inflation of q.\n\n## 3. Fully explicit base flow; no PSD selection oracle\n\nThe base is an explicitly prescribed array. It is NOT asserted PSD on the\nactual positive-epsilon word. A closed finite sum for all its coefficients\nis supplied, together with identities establishing their signs and flow.\n\nFinite summation of H_j gives\n\n    v_h=a/(c-2),\n    A_j=v_h c^j+(1-v_h)2^j,\n    t_j=(c-2)/[1+(1/v_h-1)(2/c)^j].                   (9)\n\nHere0<v_h<1 since c(c-2)>1>beta. The ratios increase to c-2.\nIn particular t_0,t_1<t_star<t_2, and for j>=2,\n\n    0<t_j-t_star<c-lambda=lambda epsilon, t_j<1/2.     (10)\n\nFor the signs at j=1,2, direct physical substitution gives\nlambda d_1-A_1=epsilon[-lambda+2/(lambda(1+epsilon))]<0 and\nc(lambda d_2-A_2)=epsilon[(lambda+4)-(4lambda+2)epsilon\n                                      -(5lambda+2)epsilon^2]>0.\nThe last bracket exceeds5 using12/5<lambda<5/2 and epsilon<=1/6144.\n\nDefine the complete primitive array Lambda^j on indices0..n_j.\nAt a join H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+d_j,H_j), with bridge p and terminal z,\nembed the left array with weight1, the right with weight k_j, and add\n\n    u_j h_i at i->p, v_j h_i at i->z for every right i<z,\n    w_j at z->p, z_j at p->z,\n    u_j=2lambda,\n    v_j=[u_j d_j+2(A_j+d_j)]/A_j,\n    k_j=1+(u_j+v_j)/2=2+lambda+(lambda+1)t_j,\n    z_j=2A_j,\n    w_j=4A_j+2d_j-u_j(A_j-1).                        (11)\n\nLambda^0=0. All unlisted rows are zero; repeated rows are summed.\nThis is also a closed finite coefficient formula: sum(11)'s four row\ntypes over every dyadic internal node, multiplied by the product of\nk_l over precisely those ancestors in whose RIGHT child the node lies.\nLeft ancestors contribute1. Thus each coefficient is a specified finite\nsum of products of(9)–(11), with no unknown optimization or matrix choice.\n\nEvery coefficient is nonnegative. The first four scalar quantities are\npositive. Also w_j=2lambda+2(d_j-A_j/lambda): j>=2 follows from(10),\nj=0 has w_0=4+2a, and j=1 follows from A_1<lambda, giving w_1>2lambda-2.\nThe identities u_j+v_j=2(k_j-1) and\nu_j(A_j-1)+w_j-z_j=2(A_j+d_j) give divergence -2h_i at each\nnonterminal original query and2(A_(j+1)-1) at its terminal.\n\nPut\n\n    V_j=2sum_(i<n_j)h_i(f_i-f_(n_j))\n       +sum_(i<n_j)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2\n       -A_j(A_j-1)||g_(n_j)||^2,\n    Psrc_j=V_j+sum Lambda^j_iv Q_iv.\n\nAt one original join let r=sum_(i right,i<z)h_i g_i,\nY=r-(A_j-1)g_z and Z=g_p-g_z. Direct expansion yields\n\n    Psrc_(j+1)=Psrc_j^left+k_j Psrc_j^right\n       +(t_star-t_j)[(lambda+1)||Y||^2\n          -2lambda A_j<Y,Z>+A_j^2(2+t_star+t_j)||Z||^2]. (12)\n\nTo audit all new terms, their four gradient polynomials before weighting\nare, in(11)'s order,\n\n    d_j<r,g_p>+||r||^2/2+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2/2\n                                      +(A_j-1)||g_p||^2/2;\n    -||r||^2/2+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2/2\n                                      +(A_j-1)||g_z||^2/2-<r,g_z>;\n    d_j<g_z,g_p>+<g_z,r>+(||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2)/2;\n    -(d_j+1/2)||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2/2-<g_p,r>-<g_p,g_z>.\n\nTheir r^2,r*g_p,r*g_z coefficients are(u_j-v_j)/2,\nu_j d_j-z_j and w_j-v_j. Adding V_j and the child terms yields(12).\nIts bracket is positive definite since its determinant divided by A_j^2\nis(lambda+1)(2+t_star+t_j)-lambda^2>=lambda>0.\nEquation(12) is an identity on ALL original gradients; at positive\nepsilon it is not an induction that child matrices are PSD.\n\nThe full base objective array Kbase^j, mubase^j has empty base\nKbase^0=0, mubase^0_0=2, xiCert=0. At the same join write\nA=A_j-1, bjoin=1+d_j, D=D_(j+1), D_c=D_j,\ns_j=(A+bjoin)/(3A+2bjoin), theta_j=1-s_j. Put\n\n    Kbase_(L_l,L_v)=Lambda^j_lv/D,\n    Kbase_(R_i,R_v)=Kbase^j_iv,\n    Kbase_(R_i,p)=s_j mubase^j_i,\n    mubase_(L_l)=2h_l/D for l<n_j,\n    mubase_p=2s_j-2A/D,\n    mubase_(R_i)=theta_j mubase^j_i.                  (13)\n\nAll other finite rows and every incoming optimum row are zero.\nHere L_l=l, p=L_(n_j), R_i=n_j+1+i, and R_(n_j) is the terminal.\nFor A=0, s_0=1/2; otherwise1/3<s_j<1/2. The bridge optimum row is\npositive because(A+bjoin)D-A(3A+2bjoin)\n=A^2+4A*bjoin+2bjoin^2+A+bjoin>0.\nPrimitive divergence is canceled by the left optimum rows; its terminal\ndivergence2A/D is canceled by the bridge optimum row and incoming right\nmass2s_j. Each right node preserves flow since theta_j+s_j=1.\nThis proves(1), every final sign and sum mubase=2. No row enters the\nunqueried actual terminal: this holds in the empty base and in every(13).\n\nFor an explicit unrolled original-index version at depth r, the right\nspine child of depth l has offset o_l=2^r-2^l and bridge\np_l=n_r-2^(l-1), 1<=l<=r. Sum Lambda^(l-1)/D_l on its entire left\nquery block[o_l,p_l], and add s_(l-1)mubase^(l-1)_i at each original\nright query i->p_l, INCLUDING the final query. These are every finite\nrow. Outgoing optimum rows on this left block are\n\n    (2h_i/D_l) product_(t=l)^(r-1)theta_t for i<p_l,\n    [2s_(l-1)-2(A_(l-1)-1)/D_l] product_(t=l)^(r-1)theta_t at p_l,\n\nand at n_r they are2 product_(t=0)^(r-1)theta_t.\nThe same finite formula inside the child gives every mubase used in the\nspine addition. All coefficients are thus closed finite products/sums.\n\nDefine astar_j=product_(t=0)^(j-1)theta_t, astar_0=1.\nThe ENTIRE outgoing terminal probability of this base is explicitly\n\n    alpha_star^base=astar_r,\n    alpha_(p_l)^base=s_(l-1)astar_(l-1), 1<=l<=r,\n    alpha_i^base=0 at every other finite prefix index.  (14)\n\nThey sum to1 since s_j astar_j=astar_j-astar_(j+1).\nThere is no assumed optimum re-selection in(14).\n\nFinally let r_R=sum_i mubase^j_i g_(R_i),\nx_p=x_0-sum_(l<n_j)h_l g_(L_l),\nRchild_R=Cbase^j(g_R)-D_c||r_R||^2/4, and\nkappa_j=D_c-D theta_j^2. The entire base identity is\n\n    Mbase_(j+1)(1/D)=Psrc_j(g_L)/D+Rchild_R\n       +||x_p+(A-D s_j)g_p-(D theta_j/2)r_R||^2/D\n       +(kappa_j/4)||r_R-2g_p||^2.                   (15)\n\nHere Cbase is the gradient matrix from(3) for(13). To check the\ninitial and every mixed term, the polynomial after source extraction is\n||x_p||^2/D+2(A/D-s_j)<x_p,g_p>+(A^2/D+2s_j)||g_p||^2\n-theta_j<x_p,r_R>+(bjoin-s_j)<g_p,r_R>+D_c||r_R||^2/4.\nThe scalar equality s_j(3A+2bjoin)=A+bjoin completes it to(15).\nThis also proves base contact Cbase e=D mubase/2: the primitive common\ngradient derivatives vanish by(12), and the displayed squares vanish\nat x_0=D,g_i=1 with the inherited child contact identity. Empty base\ncontact starts the induction. Neither Rchild_R nor kappa_j is presumed\nnonnegative on the positive-epsilon word.\n\n## 4. Constructed nonlocal terminal reroute and complete prefix flow\n\nAt actual depth r>=3 choose ONE of the explicit distributions below and\nrhoRer=epsilon/4. For r<=2 set rhoRer=0. Write n=n_r, D=D_r,\nalpha_i=alpha_i^base for i<n, astar=astar_r, Tmass=1-astar.\nLet piRer_i be a probability on ALL finite original prefix indices0..n-1.\nDefine every FINAL row by\n\n    alpha_star^new=astar,\n    alpha_i^new=(1-rhoRer)alpha_i+rhoRer*Tmass*piRer_i,\n    Knew_(n,i)=2alpha_i^new, nunew_n=2astar,\n    Knew_(i,n)=0 for every i<n,\n    Knew_(i,j)=Kbase_(i,j)+2rhoRer*piRer_i*alpha_j,\n                         i,j<n, i!=j,\n    nunew_i=mubase_i for i<n, xiCertnew_i=0.          (16)\n\nAll unlisted rows are zero. In particular the star is retained and has\nno incoming direction in these laws, as explicitly chosen here.\nEvery row is nonnegative. The only decrement is rhoRer of each actual\noriginal terminal finite edge, which is paid by that SAME edge.\nNo prefix row is decremented. The self-pair i=j is skipped; its path\nkeeps its old terminal edge rather than creating a diagonal residual.\n\nThe construction splits each old path n->j into n->i->j with probability\nrhoRer*piRer_i. Prefix added divergence is exactly\n2rhoRer(Tmass*piRer_i-alpha_i); the terminal incoming change at i is\nthe same quantity. Thus(1) holds at EVERY original finite node, and the\nprefix flow of fact1252b8823bb5a290 is exactly out_i^pre-in_i^pre\n=2alpha_i^new. Terminal outgoing mass is exactly2. This proves full\nvalue flow without adopting a previous unverified flow identity.\n\nFor every original ordered triple, direct expansion gives\n\n    Q_(n,i)+Q_(i,j)-Q_(n,j)=<g_n-g_i,y_i-y_j>,\n    y_i=x_i-g_i.                                    (17)\n\nThe value terms cancel. The position terms are\n<g_n-g_i,x_i-x_j>; the difference-square terms are\n-<g_n-g_i,g_i-g_j>, proving(17) on the ENTIRE original formal space.\nConsequently the full law is the explicit polynomial\n\n    Mnew(q)=Mbase(q)+DeltaM,\n    DeltaM=2rhoRer sum_(i,j<n)piRer_i alpha_j<g_n-g_i,y_i-y_j>.\n\nSet ybar=sum_(j<n)alpha_j y_j and gbar=sum_(i<n)piRer_i g_i.\nExpanding that finite sum, without assuming independence pays a square,\n\n    DeltaM=2rhoRer[<g_n,Tmass sum_i piRer_i y_i-ybar>\n                  -Tmass sum_i piRer_i<g_i,y_i>+<gbar,ybar>]. (18)\n\nEquations(3),(13),(16) specify every coefficient, including every new\noff-diagonal. Equation(18) is a bilinear identity, NOT a PSD claim.\n\nThe terminal-square fact applies to these concrete rows:\n\n    V_new=x_n-astar*y_star-sum_i alpha_i^new y_i,\n    R_new=q||x_0||^2-2sum_i alpha_i^new(f_i-||g_i||^2/2)\n                 -||V_new||^2+sum_(i!=j in J_pre)lambda^pre_new_ij Q_ij,\n    Mnew=||g_n-V_new||^2+R_new.                       (19)\n\nHere y_star=0 and lambda^pre_new consists EXACTLY of(16)'s prefix K\nand nu, with zero incoming optimum rows. This is the complete original\nprefix remainder, not an oracle or a conditional-covariance certificate.\nIts cumulative terminal probabilities w_t are the finite sums of(16),\nand V_new=astar*x_0-sum_(t<n)(h_t w_t-alpha_t^new)g_t as in the\naccepted fact. All original gradients remain independent.\n\nInitial mixed coefficients nu are unchanged. The contact defect is\nnonzero in general and paid, not suppressed. Define\nPbar=sum_(j<n)alpha_j Ppos_j, Ppi=sum_i piRer_i Ppos_i. Differentiating\n(18) at a common gradient, where g_n-g_i=0 and y_i-y_j=Ppos_j-Ppos_i,\ngives the ENTIRE changed defect\n\n    dcontact_i=rhoRer*piRer_i(Tmass*Ppos_i-Pbar), i<n,\n    dcontact_n=rhoRer(Pbar-Tmass*Ppi).                 (20)\n\nThe components sum to zero. Base contact was proved after(15), so(20)\nis the full defect, not just one contribution. Substitute csum=D nu/2\n+dcontact into(8): every transverse vector pays the exact square(20),\nwith coefficient1/[D(zeta-1)], in addition to the complete common\ncharge. Huber and common mixed prices are unchanged: Hprice=0 and the\ncommon block is [[zeta/D,-1],[-1,D]], with determinant zeta-1.\nThe initial-point Schur charge for every original gradient is still\nnu nu^T/(4q), but its gradient matrix is now the FULL(18).\nNo renewable local objective allowance is used.\n\nThese formulas are polynomial identities even with zero physical steps;\nthey impose no equality on repeated formal gradients. Empty r=0 is the\naccepted direct certificate lambda_(0,star)=2, M=||x_0-g_0||^2.\nNo bound is claimed for a different word or a compressed horizon.\n\n## 5. Three concrete laws and full small/Silver calibrations\n\nFor a nonempty depth-r word, define the following CLOSED distributions:\n\n    Law A: piRer_i=1/n_r, Z_A=n_r.\n\n    Law B: piRer_i=h_i/S_r, Z_B=S_r.\n\n    Law C: Lmask=65536,\n           vmask_i=h_i+Lmask*1_(i congruent3 modulo4),\n           Z_C=S_r+Lmask*floor(n_r/4),\n           piRer_i=vmask_i/Z_C.                     (21)\n\nFor r>=2, floor(n_r/4)=2^(r-2)-1. All three distributions sum to1.\nThe actual H_r has positive steps, so all three give strictly positive\nnew alpha_i at EVERY finite prefix query when r>=3. Their star probability\nis precisely astar_r. Law C fixes its mask and coefficient once; it does\nnot solve for a feasible distribution, use a running budget, or retune\nthe old finite cycles or adjacent mixture. Every original prefix edge\nis declared by(16), including remote edges from each i to EVERY nonzero\nbase terminal destination p_l. The construction has actual remote support.\n\nAt r<=2, rhoRer=0, so the complete actual H2 calibration is the base,\nwith ALL original initial mixed terms. Put b=1+a; then7/5<b<sqrt(2)<10/7.\nAt H1, (11) and(13) give\n\n    Psrc_1=(2-b^2)||g_0-g_1||^2,\n    Mbase_1(1/D_1)=||x_0-D_1(g_0+g_1)/2||^2/D_1\n                              +(3-2b)||g_0-g_1||^2/4. (22)\n\nBoth remainders are PSD. At H2 the physical bridge is2-epsilon,\nD_1>19/5, D_2<75/7, and\ntheta_1=(2b+2-epsilon)/(3b+4-2epsilon)<59/100.\nThe last ratio increases in b and decreases in the physical bridge;\nuse b<10/7 and2-epsilon>199/100 to prove the bound by cross multiplication.\nThen D_2 theta_1^2<15/4 and kappa_1>1/20.\nThe full(15), its entire H1 Schur remainder from(22), and Psrc_1 prove\nfull H2 PSD. These are original-vector calculations, not quotient tests.\n\nAt epsilon=0 every rhoRer is zero. Also t_j=t_star and Psrc_j=0 by(12).\nWriting u_star=1/lambda, A=A_j-1 and bjoin=1+u_star(A+1), expansion gives\n\n    (3A+2bjoin)^2*kappa_j\n      =2(1-u_star)A^3+(6-8u_star)A^2\n       +(1+u_star)(8u_star-2)A+(1+u_star)^2(1-2u_star)>0. (23)\n\nEach coefficient is positive since1/4<u_star<1/2. Starting with the\nempty square, (15) and base contact give full Silver PSD by induction:\nthe ENTIRE child Schur remainder is PSD once its full matrix is PSD.\nThis use of child PSD is confined to the proved epsilon=0 calibration.\nNo positive-epsilon child PSD is assumed anywhere below.\n\n## 6. Original old H4 plane and exact all-depth base restriction\n\nWrite Wloc=A_2+d_2, Uloc=A_2+d_3, rloc=Uloc/Wloc,\nEdef=(lambda d_2-A_2)(A_2+d_2/lambda)>0 and delta3=t_3-t_star>0.\nChoose the one-dimensional ORIGINAL formal-vector test\n\n    g_0,...,g_3=u, g_4,...,g_7=v0,\n    g_8,...,g_11=w, g_12,...,g_15=0,\n    Wloc(u+w)+Uloc*v0=0.                            (24)\n\nLet N be this two-dimensional zero-displacement plane; it chooses values\nof independent test vectors and identifies no formal coordinates.\nEquation(12) at its three actual joins gives EXACTLY\n\n    P_N=-Edef(u-v0)^2-k_3 Edef*w^2\n       -delta3[(lambda+1)Wloc^2*w^2\n          -2lambda A_3 Wloc*w*v0+A_3^2(2+t_star+t_3)*v0^2]<0 (25)\n\nat every nonzero point of N. The left H3 has Y=0,Z=u-v0;\nthe right H3 contributes -Edef*w^2. The final bracket is positive\ndefinite by(12), so strict negativity follows also when either part vanishes.\n\nPlace(24) in the FIRST actual H4 of H_m, set x_0=0 and EVERY other\noriginal gradient, INCLUDING g_n and the root bridge, to zero. Its zero\nterminal and displacement make every later source Y,Z zero on the all-left\nembedding, whose weight is1. The final(15) has zero boundary and right\nvariables. Hence, for every m>=5 and every real q,\n\n    D_m Mbase_m(q)|N=P_N.                            (26)\n\nEvery base terminal destination p_l lies after this active block when\nm>=6. Its gradient and position are zero. Therefore ybar=0 on N, and\n(18) gives the exact whole-word restriction for EACH distribution\n\n    D_m Mnew(q)|N=P_N+(2rhoRer*Tmass*D_m/Z)*Q_weight,\n    Q_weight=sum_(i=0)^11 weight_i(g_i^2-g_i*x_i),     (27)\n\nwhere weight_i is1,h_i, or vmask_i, and Z is the corresponding(21).\nNo child allowance or omitted terminal term appears in(27).\n\nFor physical weights and any zero-displacement test,\n\n    sum h_i(g_i^2-g_i*x_i)=sum h_i(1-h_i/2)g_i^2      (28)\n\nat x_0=0. Indeed sum h_i g_i=0, so\nsum_(t<i)h_i h_t g_i g_t=-(sum h_i^2 g_i^2)/2.\nThis is an exact identity in the selected original vector, not a numerical\ntrajectory or an assumption about legal sampled values.\n\n## 7. Laws A and B: early exact failures\n\nUse u=1,v0=0,w=-1 in(24). It has zero displacement and every retained\noptimum, terminal and inherited initial contribution already included.\nWithin each active four-query block the local prefixes are\n0,b,b+2-epsilon,2b+2-epsilon, with total physical displacement Wloc.\nFor Law A this gives\n\n    Q_uniform=8+2[4b+2(2-epsilon)]-4Wloc=4(1-d_2)<0. (29)\n\nFor Law B, (28) gives\n\n    Q_physical=2Wloc-[2b^2+(2-epsilon)^2+(1+d_2)^2]<0. (30)\n\nFor example Wloc<826/100, b>7/5, 2-epsilon>199/100, d_2>12/5\ngive Q_physical<1652/100-98/25-39601/10000-289/25<-29/10.\nThe elementary parameter bounds are proved in Section9 below.\nCombining(25),(27),(29),(30), BOTH complete laws fail on this original\ndirection for every real q and every actual m>=1536. All signs and\nvalue flows remain valid; the gradient matrix does not.\n\nFor Law A the negative added curvature is exactly\n(8rhoRer*Tmass*D_m/n_m)(1-d_2) after D_m normalization.\nFor Law B it is exactly(2rhoRer*Tmass*D_m/S_m)Q_physical.\nThus the actual whole-word cost is displayed, without reassigning it to\na new local allowance. Section10 also supplies the separate actual root\nscreen: Law A loses the right-first diagonal; Law B passes that two-query\nplane but its independent early failure(30) persists.\n\n## 8. Quantitative growth and base root entries without child PSD\n\nWe need explicit original-coordinate root entries to screen Law C before\nthe long lift. No child PSD estimate is used. From c(c-2)\n=1+2(lambda+1)epsilon+lambda^2 epsilon^2 and lambda<5/2,\n\n    v_h=(1-epsilon)/[c(c-2)]>1-9epsilon>255/256.       (31)\n\nFor the first inequality compare with1+7epsilon+7epsilon^2;\n(1-9epsilon)(1+7epsilon+7epsilon^2)\n=1-2epsilon-56epsilon^2-63epsilon^3<1-epsilon.\nFor every j>=1533, the third binomial term gives\n\n    A_j/2^j>(255/256)(6/5)^j\n       >(255/256) j(j-1)(j-2)/750>4000000.           (32)\n\nHere j,j-1,j-2>1500, and1500^3/750=4500000.\nIn particular S_m>1000000*2^m. These are symbolic inequalities, not\nan executed recurrence, scalar replay, or benchmark computation.\n\nLet pterm_j denote Psrc_j with only its terminal gradient equal1.\nSubstituting Y=-(A_j-1),Z=-1 into(12) gives\n\n    pterm_(j+1)=k_j pterm_j+(t_star-t_j)F_j,\n    F_j=A_j^2(1+t_j)-2A_j+(lambda+1), pterm_0=0.      (33)\n\nIt can be read as the CLOSED finite sum\npterm_j=sum_(l<j)(t_star-t_l)F_l product_(v=l+1)^(j-1)k_v.\nNo value of that recurrence is executed. Since F_j=(A_j-1)^2+t_j A_j^2\n+lambda>0, pterm_1,pterm_2>=0. For j>=2,\n\n    k_j-(2+t_j)^2=(t_star-t_j)(1+t_j)<0,\n    0<F_j/A_(j+1)^2<=(1+t_j)/(2+t_j)^2<=1/4.          (34)\n\nThe middle bound uses A_j>(lambda+1)/2. Therefore the normalized value\ncan decrease by at most lambda epsilon/4 at each later level: if it is\nnegative, multiplication by k_j/(2+t_j)^2 in(0,1) makes it larger;\nif positive, the product remains positive. This proves for j<=m-1\n\n    pterm_j>=-j lambda epsilon*A_j^2/4>-5A_j^2/32.    (35)\n\nThis is a scalar diagonal LOWER estimate from an exact identity, not a\nclaim that the source or any child is PSD.\n\nSet j=m-1, Bmass=A_j, p=n_j and qright=p+1. The physical root bridge\nis h_p=1+d_j; the right-first step is b=1+a. Define astarch=astar_j,\nap=s_j astarch, T_R=1-astarch, Tmass=ap+T_R=1-theta_j astarch.\nThe base terminal destinations consist of p with mass ap and right\ndestinations with total T_R. None is qright at these depths.\nSince theta_l<=2/3, astarch<1/100 (use j>=12 and (2/3)^12<1/100).\n\nFormula(3), with the fully expanded base(13), gives EXACTLY\n\n    Cbase_pp=2s_j+[(Bmass-1)^2+pterm_j]/D_m,\n    Cbase_qright,qright=2/D_j,\n    Cbase_p,qright=b(h_p-s_j)/D_j.                   (36)\n\nFor the first entry the outgoing primitive mass at its terminal is\nA_j^2-1+pterm_j, as follows from the terminal diagonal in V_j and(3).\nThe root right-first diagonal and mixed term follow from its inherited\nchild row, split optimum weight theta_j*mubase_0, backward weight\ns_j*mubase_0 and mubase_0=2b/D_j. All inherited prefixes are retained.\n\nBy(10), d_j in((2/5)Bmass,Bmass/2), D_m<5Bmass,\nand Bmass>=2^j>1250. Equations(35),(36) then give\n\n    Cbase_pp>Bmass/6,\n    Cbase_qright,qright>1/Bmass,\n    7/25<Cbase_p,qright<2/5.                         (37)\n\nFor the first, ((27/32)Bmass^2-2Bmass+1)/(5Bmass)>Bmass/6\nwhen Bmass>1250. For the mixed upper use b<10/7 and h_p<1+Bmass/2;\nthe lower follows from b>7/5 and d_j>(2/5)Bmass.\nThus the whole root two-query GRADIENT plane, with x_0=g_n=0 and every\nother gradient zero, is positive definite already for the prescribed\nbase, even though its other directions are indefinite.\n\n## 10. Actual root bridge/right-first screens for all three laws\n\nLet omega=piRer_p and eta=piRer_qright. In the original two-query test\ng_p=u_root, g_qright=v_root, x_0=g_n=0, all other gradients zero,\ny_p=-u_root, y_qright=-h_p*u_root-v_root. From the COMPLETE base terminal\ndistribution(14),\nybar=-(ap+T_R h_p)u_root-T_R b*v_root.\nSubstitution into(18) gives exactly\n\n    DeltaC_pp=2rhoRer*omega*T_R(1-h_p),\n    DeltaC_qright,qright=2rhoRer*eta*(Tmass-b*T_R),\n    DeltaC_p,qright=rhoRer[eta*ap*(h_p-1)-omega*T_R*b]. (44)\n\nThese include every remote destination and every inherited position.\nThey are not adjacent-only approximations.\n\nFor Law A, omega=eta=1/n_m, and\n\n    Tmass-b*T_R=1-b+(b-theta_j)astarch<-(b-1)/2.\n\nUse astarch<1/100, b>7/5 and b<3/2 for this strict bound.\nIts actual right-first diagonal therefore satisfies\n\n    C_A,qright,qright<2/D_j-rhoRer*(b-1)/n_m<0.       (45)\n\nHere D_j/n_m>96m: D_j>(3/2)A_j and n_m<2^(j+1) give\nD_j/n_m>(3v_h/4)(6/5)^(m-1).\nThe third binomial term is greater than m^3/6000>300m for m>=1536,\nso the last expression exceeds200m. Then\nrhoRer*(b-1)D_j/n_m>(1/(16m))*(2/5)*96m=12/5>2.\nThe ORIGINAL assignment g_qright=1, all other vectors zero, rejects Law A\nat every q. It is a root right-first defect separate from(29).\n\nFor Law B, omega=h_p/S_m, eta=b/S_m. For Law C,\nomega=(h_p+65536)/Z_C, eta=b/Z_C, since p is3 modulo4 and qright is0.\nIn both cases Bmass>128*65536, S_m>2Bmass and h_p<1+Bmass/2 give\nomega<13/50, eta<=b/S_m. Equations(37),(44) imply\n\n    C_new,pp>Bmass*(1/6-rhoRer/3),\n    C_new,qright,qright>(1-rhoRer)/Bmass,\n    7/25-rhoRer/2<C_new,p,qright<2/5.                (46)\n\nFor the first, the decrement is2rhoRer*omega*T_R*d_j<rhoRer*Bmass/3.\nFor the second use Tmass-b*T_R>=-(b-1), b(b-1)<1, and S_m>2Bmass.\nFor the mixed entry the correction is negative since ap<1/100,\nT_R>99/100 and eta*ap*d_j<omega*T_R*b, and its magnitude is less\nrhoRer*(13/50)*(10/7)<rhoRer/2.\nBecause rhoRer<1/1000, the determinant exceeds\n\n    (1/6-1/3000)*(999/1000)-4/25\n       =(498501-480000)/3000000>0.                  (47)\n\nThus Laws B AND C pass the ENTIRE actual root two-query gradient plane,\nincluding the old direction g_p=1,g_qright=-Bmass/25. This is precisely\na screen with x_0=g_n=0. (7)\n\nThis follows by inverting [[q,-(2+X)/2],[-(2+X)/2,D+E]] and\nusing the coupling (-nu^T v/2,csum^T v). (10)\n\nFor the signs at j=1,2, direct physical substitution gives\nlambda d_1-A_1=epsilon[-lambda+2/(lambda(1+epsilon))]<0 and\nc(lambda d_2-A_2)=epsilon[(lambda+4)-(4lambda+2)epsilon\n                                      -(5lambda+2)epsilon^2]>0.\nThe last bracket exceeds5 using12/5<lambda<5/2 and epsilon<=1/6144.\n\nDefine the complete primitive array Lambda^j on indices0..n_j.\nAt a join H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+d_j,H_j), with bridge p and terminal z,\nembed the left array with weight1, the right with weight k_j, and add\n\n    u_j h_i at i->p, v_j h_i at i->z for every right i<z,\n    w_j at z->p, z_j at p->z,\n    u_j=2lambda,\n    v_j=[u_j d_j+2(A_j+d_j)]/A_j,\n    k_j=1+(u_j+v_j)/2=2+lambda+(lambda+1)t_j,\n    z_j=2A_j,\n    w_j=4A_j+2d_j-u_j(A_j-1). Adding V_j and the child terms yields(12).\nIts bracket is positive definite since its determinant divided by A_j^2\nis(lambda+1)(2+t_star+t_j)-lambda^2>=lambda>0.\nEquation(12) is an identity on ALL original gradients; at positive\nepsilon it is not an induction that child matrices are PSD.\n\nThe full base objective array Kbase^j, mubase^j has empty base\nKbase^0=0, mubase^0_0=2, xiCert=0. All coefficients are thus closed finite products/sums.\n\nDefine astar_j=product_(t=0)^(j-1)theta_t, astar_0=1.\nThe ENTIRE outgoing terminal probability of this base is explicitly\n\n    alpha_star^base=astar_r,\n    alpha_(p_l)^base=s_(l-1)astar_(l-1), 1<=l<=r,\n    alpha_i^base=0 at every other finite prefix index. This proves full\nvalue flow without adopting a previous unverified flow identity.\n\nFor every original ordered triple, direct expansion gives\n\n    Q_(n,i)+Q_(i,j)-Q_(n,j)=<g_n-g_i,y_i-y_j>,\n    y_i=x_i-g_i. All original gradients remain independent.\n\nInitial mixed coefficients nu are unchanged. The contact defect is\nnonzero in general and paid, not suppressed. Define\nPbar=sum_(j<n)alpha_j Ppos_j, Ppi=sum_i piRer_i Ppos_i. Differentiating\n(18) at a common gradient, where g_n-g_i=0 and y_i-y_j=Ppos_j-Ppos_i,\ngives the ENTIRE changed defect\n\n    dcontact_i=rhoRer*piRer_i(Tmass*Ppos_i-Pbar), i<n,\n    dcontact_n=rhoRer(Pbar-Tmass*Ppi).                 (20)\n\nThe components sum to zero. Base contact was proved after(15), so(20)\nis the full defect, not just one contribution. Substitute csum=D nu/2\n+dcontact into(8): every transverse vector pays the exact square(20),\nwith coefficient1/[D(zeta-1)], in addition to the complete common\ncharge. Huber and common mixed prices are unchanged: Hprice=0 and the\ncommon block is [[zeta/D,-1],[-1,D]], with determinant zeta-1.\nThe initial-point Schur charge for every original gradient is still\nnu nu^T/(4q), but its gradient matrix is now the FULL(18).\nNo renewable local objective allowance is used.\n\nThese formulas are polynomial identities even with zero physical steps;\nthey impose no equality on repeated formal gradients. Empty r=0 is the\naccepted direct certificate lambda_(0,star)=2, M=||x_0-g_0||^2.\nNo bound is claimed for a different word or a compressed horizon.\n\n## 5. Three concrete laws and full small/Silver calibrations\n\nFor a nonempty depth-r word, define the following CLOSED distributions:\n\n    Law A: piRer_i=1/n_r, Z_A=n_r.\n\n    Law B: piRer_i=h_i/S_r, Z_B=S_r.\n\n    Law C: Lmask=65536,\n           vmask_i=h_i+Lmask*1_(i congruent3 modulo4),\n           Z_C=S_r+Lmask*floor(n_r/4),\n           piRer_i=vmask_i/Z_C.                     (21)\n\nFor r>=2, floor(n_r/4)=2^(r-2)-1. All three distributions sum to1.\nThe actual H_r has positive steps, so all three give strictly positive\nnew alpha_i at EVERY finite prefix query when r>=3. Their star probability\nis precisely astar_r. Law C fixes its mask and coefficient once; it does\nnot solve for a feasible distribution, use a running budget, or retune\nthe old finite cycles or adjacent mixture. Every original prefix edge\nis declared by(16), including remote edges from each i to EVERY nonzero\nbase terminal destination p_l. The construction has actual remote support.\n\nAt r<=2, rhoRer=0, so the complete actual H2 calibration is the base,\nwith ALL original initial mixed terms. Put b=1+a; then7/5<b<sqrt(2)<10/7.\nAt H1, (11) and(13) give\n\n    Psrc_1=(2-b^2)||g_0-g_1||^2,\n    Mbase_1(1/D_1)=||x_0-D_1(g_0+g_1)/2||^2/D_1\n                              +(3-2b)||g_0-g_1||^2/4. (22)\n\nBoth remainders are PSD. At H2 the physical bridge is2-epsilon,\nD_1>19/5, D_2<75/7, and\ntheta_1=(2b+2-epsilon)/(3b+4-2epsilon)<59/100.\nThe last ratio increases in b and decreases in the physical bridge;\nuse b<10/7 and2-epsilon>199/100 to prove the bound by cross multiplication.\nThen D_2 theta_1^2<15/4 and kappa_1>1/20.\nThe full(15), its entire H1 Schur remainder from(22), and Psrc_1 prove\nfull H2 PSD. These are original-vector calculations, not quotient tests.\n\nAt epsilon=0 every rhoRer is zero. Also t_j=t_star and Psrc_j=0 by(12).\nWriting u_star=1/lambda, A=A_j-1 and bjoin=1+u_star(A+1), expansion gives\n\n    (3A+2bjoin)^2*kappa_j\n      =2(1-u_star)A^3+(6-8u_star)A^2\n       +(1+u_star)(8u_star-2)A+(1+u_star)^2(1-2u_star)>0. (23)\n\nEach coefficient is positive since1/4<u_star<1/2. Starting with the\nempty square, (15) and base contact give full Silver PSD by induction:\nthe ENTIRE child Schur remainder is PSD once its full matrix is PSD.\nThis use of child PSD is confined to the proved epsilon=0 calibration.\nNo positive-epsilon child PSD is assumed anywhere below.\n\n## 6. Original old H4 plane and exact all-depth base restriction\n\nWrite Wloc=A_2+d_2, Uloc=A_2+d_3, rloc=Uloc/Wloc,\nEdef=(lambda d_2-A_2)(A_2+d_2/lambda)>0 and delta3=t_3-t_star>0.\nChoose the one-dimensional ORIGINAL formal-vector test\n\n    g_0,...,g_3=u, g_4,...,g_7=v0,\n    g_8,...,g_11=w, g_12,...,g_15=0,\n    Wloc(u+w)+Uloc*v0=0.                            (24)\n\nLet N be this two-dimensional zero-displacement plane; it chooses values\nof independent test vectors and identifies no formal coordinates.\nEquation(12) at its three actual joins gives EXACTLY\n\n    P_N=-Edef(u-v0)^2-k_3 Edef*w^2\n       -delta3[(lambda+1)Wloc^2*w^2\n          -2lambda A_3 Wloc*w*v0+A_3^2(2+t_star+t_3)*v0^2]<0 (25)\n\nat every nonzero point of N. The left H3 has Y=0,Z=u-v0;\nthe right H3 contributes -Edef*w^2. The final bracket is positive\ndefinite by(12), so strict negativity follows also when either part vanishes.\n\nPlace(24) in the FIRST actual H4 of H_m, set x_0=0 and EVERY other\noriginal gradient, INCLUDING g_n and the root bridge, to zero. Its zero\nterminal and displacement make every later source Y,Z zero on the all-left\nembedding, whose weight is1. The final(15) has zero boundary and right\nvariables. Hence, for every m>=5 and every real q,\n\n    D_m Mbase_m(q)|N=P_N.                            (26)\n\nEvery base terminal destination p_l lies after this active block when\nm>=6. Its gradient and position are zero. Therefore ybar=0 on N, and\n(18) gives the exact whole-word restriction for EACH distribution\n\n    D_m Mnew(q)|N=P_N+(2rhoRer*Tmass*D_m/Z)*Q_weight,\n    Q_weight=sum_(i=0)^11 weight_i(g_i^2-g_i*x_i),     (27)\n\nwhere weight_i is1,h_i, or vmask_i, and Z is the corresponding(21).\nNo child allowance or omitted terminal term appears in(27).\n\nFor physical weights and any zero-displacement test,\n\n    sum h_i(g_i^2-g_i*x_i)=sum h_i(1-h_i/2)g_i^2      (28)\n\nat x_0=0. Indeed sum h_i g_i=0, so\nsum_(t<i)h_i h_t g_i g_t=-(sum h_i^2 g_i^2)/2.\nThis is an exact identity in the selected original vector, not a numerical\ntrajectory or an assumption about legal sampled values.\n\n## 7. Laws A and B: early exact failures\n\nUse u=1,v0=0,w=-1 in(24). It has zero displacement and every retained\noptimum, terminal and inherited initial contribution already included.\nWithin each active four-query block the local prefixes are\n0,b,b+2-epsilon,2b+2-epsilon, with total physical displacement Wloc.\nFor Law A this gives\n\n    Q_uniform=8+2[4b+2(2-epsilon)]-4Wloc=4(1-d_2)<0. (29)\n\nFor Law B, (28) gives\n\n    Q_physical=2Wloc-[2b^2+(2-epsilon)^2+(1+d_2)^2]<0. (30)\n\nFor example Wloc<826/100, b>7/5, 2-epsilon>199/100, d_2>12/5\ngive Q_physical<1652/100-98/25-39601/10000-289/25<-29/10.\nThe elementary parameter bounds are proved in Section9 below.\nCombining(25),(27),(29),(30), BOTH complete laws fail on this original\ndirection for every real q and every actual m>=1536. All signs and\nvalue flows remain valid; the gradient matrix does not.\n\nFor Law A the negative added curvature is exactly\n(8rhoRer*Tmass*D_m/n_m)(1-d_2) after D_m normalization.\nFor Law B it is exactly(2rhoRer*Tmass*D_m/S_m)Q_physical.\nThus the actual whole-word cost is displayed, without reassigning it to\na new local allowance. Section10 also supplies the separate actual root\nscreen: Law A loses the right-first diagonal; Law B passes that two-query\nplane but its independent early failure(30) persists.\n\n## 8. Quantitative growth and base root entries without child PSD\n\nWe need explicit original-coordinate root entries to screen Law C before\nthe long lift. No child PSD estimate is used. From c(c-2)\n=1+2(lambda+1)epsilon+lambda^2 epsilon^2 and lambda<5/2,\n\n    v_h=(1-epsilon)/[c(c-2)]>1-9epsilon>255/256.       (31)\n\nFor the first inequality compare with1+7epsilon+7epsilon^2;\n(1-9epsilon)(1+7epsilon+7epsilon^2)\n=1-2epsilon-56epsilon^2-63epsilon^3<1-epsilon.\nFor every j>=1533, the third binomial term gives\n\n    A_j/2^j>(255/256)(6/5)^j\n       >(255/256) j(j-1)(j-2)/750>4000000.           (32)\n\nHere j,j-1,j-2>1500, and1500^3/750=4500000.\nIn particular S_m>1000000*2^m. These are symbolic inequalities, not\nan executed recurrence, scalar replay, or benchmark computation.\n\nLet pterm_j denote Psrc_j with only its terminal gradient equal1.\nSubstituting Y=-(A_j-1),Z=-1 into(12) gives\n\n    pterm_(j+1)=k_j pterm_j+(t_star-t_j)F_j,\n    F_j=A_j^2(1+t_j)-2A_j+(lambda+1), pterm_0=0.      (33)\n\nIt can be read as the CLOSED finite sum\npterm_j=sum_(l<j)(t_star-t_l)F_l product_(v=l+1)^(j-1)k_v.\nNo value of that recurrence is executed. Since F_j=(A_j-1)^2+t_j A_j^2\n+lambda>0, pterm_1,pterm_2>=0. For j>=2,\n\n    k_j-(2+t_j)^2=(t_star-t_j)(1+t_j)<0,\n    0<F_j/A_(j+1)^2<=(1+t_j)/(2+t_j)^2<=1/4.          (34)\n\nThe middle bound uses A_j>(lambda+1)/2. Therefore the normalized value\ncan decrease by at most lambda epsilon/4 at each later level: if it is\nnegative, multiplication by k_j/(2+t_j)^2 in(0,1) makes it larger;\nif positive, the product remains positive. This proves for j<=m-1\n\n    pterm_j>=-j lambda epsilon*A_j^2/4>-5A_j^2/32.    (35)\n\nThis is a scalar diagonal LOWER estimate from an exact identity, not a\nclaim that the source or any child is PSD.\n\nSet j=m-1, Bmass=A_j, p=n_j and qright=p+1. The physical root bridge\nis h_p=1+d_j; the right-first step is b=1+a. Define astarch=astar_j,\nap=s_j astarch, T_R=1-astarch, Tmass=ap+T_R=1-theta_j astarch.\nThe base terminal destinations consist of p with mass ap and right\ndestinations with total T_R. None is qright at these depths.\nSince theta_l<=2/3, astarch<1/100 (use j>=12 and (2/3)^12<1/100).\n\nFormula(3), with the fully expanded base(13), gives EXACTLY\n\n    Cbase_pp=2s_j+[(Bmass-1)^2+pterm_j]/D_m,\n    Cbase_qright,qright=2/D_j,\n    Cbase_p,qright=b(h_p-s_j)/D_j.                   (36)\n\nFor the first entry the outgoing primitive mass at its terminal is\nA_j^2-1+pterm_j, as follows from the terminal diagonal in V_j and(3).\nThe root right-first diagonal and mixed term follow from its inherited\nchild row, split optimum weight theta_j*mubase_0, backward weight\ns_j*mubase_0 and mubase_0=2b/D_j. All inherited prefixes are retained.\n\nBy(10), d_j in((2/5)Bmass,Bmass/2), D_m<5Bmass,\nand Bmass>=2^j>1250. Equations(35),(36) then give\n\n    Cbase_pp>Bmass/6,\n    Cbase_qright,qright>1/Bmass,\n    7/25<Cbase_p,qright<2/5.                         (37)\n\nFor the first, ((27/32)Bmass^2-2Bmass+1)/(5Bmass)>Bmass/6\nwhen Bmass>1250. For the mixed upper use b<10/7 and h_p<1+Bmass/2;\nthe lower follows from b>7/5 and d_j>(2/5)Bmass.\nThus the whole root two-query GRADIENT plane, with x_0=g_n=0 and every\nother gradient zero, is positive definite already for the prescribed\nbase, even though its other directions are indefinite.\n\n## 9. Law C passes the entire old N plane at the actual word\n\nThe fixed subsidy is large enough to pay this screen, with no allowance\nrenewed at higher levels. Let normN=u^2+v0^2+w^2 on(24).\nOn a zero-displacement N vector, define Q_mask using unit weights only\nat original queries3,7,11; query15 has zero gradient. Since\nx_3=-(A_2-1)u, x_7=-Wloc*u-(A_2-1)v0,\nx_11=-Wloc*u-Uloc*v0-(A_2-1)w,\n\n    Q_mask=A_2(u^2+v0^2+w^2)+Wloc*u(v0+w)+Uloc*v0*w.\n\nUsing u=-w-rloc*v0,\n\n    Q_mask=(A_2-d_2)w^2+(2A_2 rloc-Wloc)w*v0\n                                +(A_2 rloc^2-d_3)v0^2. (38)\n\nWe prove a uniform lower bound, rather than checking one lucky point.\nThe actual epsilon<=1/6144 implies\n\n    582/100<A_2<584/100, 241/100<d_2<242/100,\n    582/100<d_3<584/100, 141/100<rloc<142/100.          (39)\n\nFor explicit verification use707/500<sqrt(2)<283/200 and\nb=sqrt(2)-2epsilon/[lambda(1+epsilon)] to get\n5827/1000<A_2=3+2b-epsilon<583/100.\nAlso d_2=lambda(1-epsilon^2) lies in(2413/1000,2415/1000), and\nd_3=lambda^2(1-epsilon^2)(1+epsilon) lies in(5827/1000,584/100).\nThese sharper intervals give\nrloc>(2*5827/1000)/(583/100+2415/1000)>141/100 and\nrloc<(583/100+584/100)/(5827/1000+2413/1000)<142/100.\n\nSubtract normN/8= w^2/4+rloc*w*v0/4+(rloc^2+1)v0^2/8\nfrom(38). Its w^2 coefficient exceeds63/20; its v0^2 coefficient\nexceeds53/10; the absolute value of its cross coefficient is less81/10.\nThese follow by direct insertion of(39):\n\n    A_2-d_2-1/4>5.82-2.42-.25=3.15,\n    A_2 rloc^2-d_3-(rloc^2+1)/8\n       >5.82*(1.41)^2-5.84-[(1.42)^2+1]/8>5.3,\n    0<2A_2 rloc-Wloc-rloc/4<8.1.\n\nThe determinant is positive because\n(63/20)(53/10)=3339/200>6561/400=(81/10)^2/4.\nThus the entire plane has\n\n    Q_mask>normN/8.                                 (40)\n\nThe strict inequality in(40) is for nonzero N vectors; both sides are\nzero at the zero vector. The strict bounds in(41) below have the same\nnonzero-vector convention.\n\nThe physical part Q_physical from(28) is greater than -100normN:\neach four-query block has steps below7, so the sum of its four negative\nterms -h_i^2/2 exceeds -98; positive terms may be discarded.\nThe source base loss is also fully bounded. In(25), Edef<24epsilon,\nk_3<7, delta3<(5/2)epsilon, Wloc<9, A_3<15,\nlambda+1<4, 2+t_star+t_3<3. The absolute cross term is at most\nlambda A_3 Wloc(w^2+v0^2)<338(w^2+v0^2).\nTogether these give\n\n    P_N>-3000epsilon*normN,\n    Q_vmask=Q_physical+65536Q_mask>8092normN.          (41)\n\nBy(32), Z_C=S_m+65536(2^(m-2)-1)<(33/32)S_m;\nTmass>99/100. Since D_m>2S_m and rhoRer=epsilon/4,\n\n    2rhoRer*Tmass*D_m/Z_C\n       >(epsilon/2)(99/100)(64/33)>(3/4)epsilon.       (42)\n\nCombining(27),(41),(42), at EVERY nonzero original N vector,\n\n    D_m M_C(q)|N>3069epsilon*normN>0.                (43)\n\nThis pays the WHOLE old two-dimensional screen with the actual global\nnormalization, not a reset child normalizer. It does not assert full PSD.\nIn particular the large fixed subsidy may create additional isolated\nquery defects; all those entries remain explicitly in(3),(16),(18).\nThe exact deeper negative direction below is valid regardless of those\nadditional screens. No such direction is hidden or priced as free.\n\n## 10. Actual root bridge/right-first screens for all three laws\n\nLet omega=piRer_p and eta=piRer_qright. In the original two-query test\ng_p=u_root, g_qright=v_root, x_0=g_n=0, all other gradients zero,\ny_p=-u_root, y_qright=-h_p*u_root-v_root. From the COMPLETE base terminal\ndistribution(14),\nybar=-(ap+T_R h_p)u_root-T_R b*v_root.\nSubstitution into(18) gives exactly\n\n    DeltaC_pp=2rhoRer*omega*T_R(1-h_p),\n    DeltaC_qright,qright=2rhoRer*eta*(Tmass-b*T_R),\n    DeltaC_p,qright=rhoRer[eta*ap*(h_p-1)-omega*T_R*b]. (44)\n\nThese include every remote destination and every inherited position.\nThey are not adjacent-only approximations.\n\nFor Law A, omega=eta=1/n_m, and\n\n    Tmass-b*T_R=1-b+(b-theta_j)astarch<-(b-1)/2.\n\nUse astarch<1/100, b>7/5 and b<3/2 for this strict bound.\nIts actual right-first diagonal therefore satisfies\n\n    C_A,qright,qright<2/D_j-rhoRer*(b-1)/n_m<0.       (45)\n\nHere D_j/n_m>96m: D_j>(3/2)A_j and n_m<2^(j+1) give\nD_j/n_m>(3v_h/4)(6/5)^(m-1).\nThe third binomial term is greater than m^3/6000>300m for m>=1536,\nso the last expression exceeds200m. Then\nrhoRer*(b-1)D_j/n_m>(1/(16m))*(2/5)*96m=12/5>2.\nThe ORIGINAL assignment g_qright=1, all other vectors zero, rejects Law A\nat every q. It is a root right-first defect separate from(29).\n\nFor Law B, omega=h_p/S_m, eta=b/S_m. For Law C,\nomega=(h_p+65536)/Z_C, eta=b/Z_C, since p is3 modulo4 and qright is0.\nIn both cases Bmass>128*65536, S_m>2Bmass and h_p<1+Bmass/2 give\nomega<13/50, eta<=b/S_m. Equations(37),(44) imply\n\n    C_new,pp>Bmass*(1/6-rhoRer/3),\n    C_new,qright,qright>(1-rhoRer)/Bmass,\n    7/25-rhoRer/2<C_new,p,qright<2/5.                (46)\n\nFor the first, the decrement is2rhoRer*omega*T_R*d_j<rhoRer*Bmass/3.\nFor the second use Tmass-b*T_R>=-(b-1), b(b-1)<1, and S_m>2Bmass.\nFor the mixed entry the correction is negative since ap<1/100,\nT_R>99/100 and eta*ap*d_j<omega*T_R*b, and its magnitude is less\nrhoRer*(13/50)*(10/7)<rhoRer/2.\nBecause rhoRer<1/1000, the determinant exceeds\n\n    (1/6-1/3000)*(999/1000)-4/25\n       =(498501-480000)/3000000>0.                  (47)\n\nThus Laws B AND C pass the ENTIRE actual root two-query gradient plane,\nincluding the old direction g_p=1,g_qright=-Bmass/25. This is precisely\na screen with x_0=g_n=0. It is not a claim about the larger terminal\nSchur plane, initial mixed directions, singular modes or full PSD.\nAll those directions continue to be represented by(3),(18),(19),(20).\n\n## 11. Law C: exact full-depth failure after both screens pass\n\nSet j=m-3>=1533 and Kblock=2^j. In the FIRST original H_(j+2)=H_(m-1),\nchoose the following original one-dimensional formal vector:\n\n    g_i=1 for0<=i<Kblock,\n    g_i=0 forKblock<=i<2Kblock,\n    g_i=-1 for2Kblock<=i<3Kblock,\n    g_i=0 for3Kblock<=i<=n_m,\n    x_0=0.                                         (48)\n\nBoth active blocks contain the physical H_j followed by its actual\nbridge1+d_j, so each has displacement Lblock=A_j+d_j, with opposite\nsign. The entire prefix displacement is zero, its terminal query is zero,\nand EVERY subsequent original query has position and gradient zero.\nNo gradients are deleted, no child parameters are reset, and (48) need\nnot be a legal primal transcript to test PSD of the full matrix.\n\nDefine E_j=(t_j-t_star)A_j^2(lambda+t_j)>0.\nFor the first H_(j+1), the all-common H_j source remainder is zero by(12),\nthe right child is zero, and its join has Y=0,Z=1. Thus its source value\nis -E_j. The third-block copy similarly has source -E_j.\nThe H_(j+2) join has Y=-Lblock,Z=0. Hence the EXACT source value is\n\n    P_big=-(1+k_(j+1))E_j\n                   -(t_(j+1)-t_star)(lambda+1)Lblock^2<0. (49)\n\nAt the final objective join (48) kills all boundary/right terms in(15).\nEvery base terminal destination p_l is at or beyond the zero terminal\nof this first H_(m-1), so ybar=0. For EVERY real q, (18) gives\n\n    D_m M_C(q;48)=P_big+(2rhoRer*Tmass*D_m/Z_C)*Q_big. (50)\n\nThis is the whole original-space action including ALL remote prefix\nrows, the optimum, terminal square and initial mixed terms.\nThe physical part of Q_big, by(28), is\n\n    Q_big,physical=2Lblock\n                      -[sum_(i<n_j)(H_j)_i^2+(1+d_j)^2]. (51)\n\nFor the mask part, every active block has Kblock/4=2^(j-2) mask queries.\nWrite P_local_i for the physical prefix inside H_j at a mask query.\nThe paired contributions of its two opposite blocks are\n\n    Q_big,mask=sum_(i congruent3 mod4,0<=i<Kblock)\n                          [2+2P_local_i-Lblock]\n                 <=2^(j-2)(A_j-d_j)<2^(j-2)A_j.      (52)\n\nIndeed P_local_i<=S_j=A_j-1, including its last bridge QUERY. This uses\none paired contribution per mask index; both active blocks are already\ncounted. It is not a per-level renewable correction.\nSince Lblock<(3/2)A_j and d_j>(2/5)A_j, (51),(52) imply\n\n    Q_big=Q_big,physical+65536Q_big,mask\n       <A_j[3+65536*2^(j-2)-(4/25)A_j].              (53)\n\nBy(32), A_j>4000000*2^j, so\n(3+65536*2^(j-2))/A_j<1/100. Consequently\n\n    Q_big<-(3/20)A_j^2.\n\nTogether with(42),(49),(50), the actual complete certificate obeys\n\n    D_m M_C(q;48)<P_big-(9/80)epsilon*A_j^2\n                         <-(9/80)epsilon*A_(m-3)^2<0 (54)\n\nfor EVERY m>=1536 and EVERY real q. In particular it fails every\nadvertised q=zeta/D_m, including1<zeta<250/237. This is an explicit\nquantitative whole-word failure. The constant mask pays the old H4 plane,\nbut its 2^j*A_j compensation cannot pay an A_j^2 physical defect at\nlarge actual j; the precise global normalization and loss are in(50)–(54).\nNo child PSD, locally replenished slack, conditional covariance payment,\nor black-box negative scalar is used.\n\n## 12. Verdict, scope and next interface\n\nLaw A has fully declared nonlocal terminal mass and legal remote prefix\nflow, but both old N and a separate root right-first coordinate are negative.\nLaw B uses physical mass, passes the actual root two-query screen, and\nstill fails old N. Law C changes the actual terminal probabilities at\nevery query and strictly pays the full old N plus the actual root plane;\nthe complete original deeper mode(48) has quantitative negative action(54).\nBoth optimum directions were retained explicitly: the incoming ones are\nchosen zero in these three laws, while all outgoing ones remain unchanged\nand sum to2. Huber, common, transverse-contact and initial mixed costs\nare given exactly in(2)–(8),(18)–(20), not treated as free.\n\nDiscard ONLY these THREE independent terminal-reroute distributions with\nthe exact base flow and rhoRer specified here. The calculation does not\nexclude destination-dependent couplings, different prefix transports,\nretaining/completing a different base, signed paid reroutes, or the full\nterminal-outgoing cone. It also does not prove impossibility of every\nfixed-mask distribution. The larger-mode mechanism suggests that a\nsuccessful payment must track actual physical mass across scales AND\norient the remote prefix flow to avoid the negative weighted energy(51).\nChanging probabilities without altering that bilinear energy is insufficient\nfor THESE laws. {"id": "3a0ebc8f9657d654", "timestamp_utc": "2026-10-02T11:03:34.076786+00:00", "author": "max2", "kind": "proof_attempt", "claim": "max2r65 COMPLETE JOINT UNVERIFIED actual binary-character capped-pyramid screen: arbitrary rank, widths, exterior carrier kappa>=1, all base-bounded initial transverse vectors, every actual full H_m query and all static complete-prefix completions. Terminal near-end exceptions and every old anchor retained; no unrestricted W upper or original-Goal result.", "evidence": "# Actual binary-character pyramid: complete projection dynamics and a scoped full-word obstruction\n\nStatus: **one complete joint analytical candidate, UNVERIFIED**. Worker max2,\nallocated invocation65 after ended baseline64, under TASK.md and shared guidance\n90760fb68a7680b7. ONE policy is selected; its parameters and initialization are\nnot additional policies. No mathematical program, numerical evaluation, replay,\noptimizer, recurrence execution, subordinate agent, or verifier call is used.\nThe original exact-profile target remains OPEN.\n\nThe policy is an actual fixed convex dictionary, not a prescribed character\ngradient history. The proof gives its real projection, every physical query,\nall original directed rows, a fixed maximal prefix completion retaining every\nold jet, complete minima and exact closest distances for both explicit\nfunctions. Its growing-rank screen is uniform in the rank and all widths.\n\nAccepted inputs: fact_id1252b8823bb5a290 for the complete finite-prefix\ninterpolation/conjugate construction and ordered interpolation necessity;\nfact_id5fd396eba75651e7 ONLY for its already accepted scalar SAME-word\nHuber/original-benchmark comparison. No spiral-class exclusion is applied to\nthis new body. Fact_idc3bfc54321ddec65 supplies the accepted example of retaining\nall old conjugate anchors, including first-query anchors on a revisited face;\nnone of its special-word performance formulas is used here. All new cone,\nthreshold, hierarchy and family-optimum arguments below are supplied jointly,\nnot imported from unverified global findings.\n\n## 1. Exact policy, real character realization, and target normalization\n\nFor a finite nonnegative physical word h=(h_0,...,h_(n-1)), let W_n(h) be the\noriginal supremum of 2(f(x_n)-min f)/(L||x_0-x_star||^2), over all finite real\ndimensions, all convex differentiable L-smooth functions with an attained\nminimum, every minimizer x_star and x_0 different from it, with the actual\nupdates x_(i+1)=x_i-(h_i/L)grad f(x_i). Thus W=2R in the project's rate\nnotation. In this document a closest-distance normalized attained payoff is\ndenoted V; no distance is denoted by the rate symbol R.\n\nChoose any integer q>=1, any positive reals b_1,...,b_q, any kappa>=1, and any\nreal vector z_0=(z_(0,1),...,z_(0,q)) satisfying |z_(0,j)|<=b_j. All choices may\ndepend on the complete known word and horizon, and are made before defining\nthe function or running GD. The ambient metric is the identity matrix on\nR^(q+1). Coordinate0 is the carrier; the other q coordinates are transverse.\n\nThe finite group is G=(Z/2Z)^q. For s=(s_1,...,s_q) in G its real coordinate\ncharacters are chi_j(s)=(-1)^(s_j). Give every group element the predetermined\nweight 2^(-q). These weights describe the full orbit; the support below is a\nmaximum over the whole orbit, not an expected gradient or expected Gram.\nEvery real slope and offset is specified by\n\n    v_s=(1,b_1 chi_1(s),...,b_q chi_q(s)),  offset_s=0,\n    D=conv({0} union {v_s:s in G}),\n    J(y)=max(0,max_(s in G)<v_s,y>)\n        =max(0,y_0+sum_(j=1)^q b_j |y_j|),\n    F(x)=max_(g in D){<x,g>-||g||^2/2},\n    x_0=(kappa,z_0).\n\nAll old support planes have height/offset zero. There is no unspecified\nfield-valued Gram: the displayed q+1 real coordinates are the realization.\nThe full dictionary has 2^q+1 listed points. Its real vertex Gram is\n\n    <v_s,v_t>=1+sum_j b_j^2 chi_j(s)chi_j(t).\n\nThis is merely a geometric identity; interpolation is proved separately.\nIts convex hull and generated cone have exact descriptions\n\n    D={(t,w):0<=t<=1, |w_j|<=b_j t for all j},\n    C={(t,w):t>=0, |w_j|<=b_j t for all j}.\n\nFor the hull description, at t>0 any w/t in the rectangular base is the\nconvex combination with coefficients\n2^(-q) product_j[1+chi_j(s)w_j/(t b_j)]. These coefficients are nonnegative\nand sum to1 by separate summation in each sign; their mean coordinates give\nw/t. Add the origin coefficient1-t for D. The case t=0 gives only zero.\nThis proves the descriptions from the full real orbit, without an abstract\nincidence or covariance assumption.\n\nThe cone is closed by its finite displayed inequalities, and its polar is\n\n    C^polar={(c,z): c+sum_j b_j |z_j|<=0}.\n\nIndeed maximizing a linear form on the base at carrier1 gives precisely\nc+sum b_j|z_j|; positive scaling tests the cone. In particular every point\nof C^polar has a nonpositive carrier coordinate. The chosen initial point\nbelongs to C, even when it lies far above the cap of D.\n\n## 2. Exhaustive projection and original word-to-face map\n\nWrite an arbitrary real query as x=(c,z). For a proposed carrier t in[0,1],\nthe closest feasible transverse coordinate is\n\n    w_j(t)=clip(z_j,[-b_j t,b_j t]).\n\nAfter this coordinate minimization the squared distance to D is\n\n    (t-c)^2+sum_j(|z_j|-b_j t)_+^2.\n\nDefine its half-derivative\n\n    Psi_x(t)=t-c-sum_j b_j(|z_j|-b_j t)_+.\n\nIt is continuous and strictly increasing: increasing t by d>0 increases\nthe first term by d and cannot increase any subtracted summand. Hence the\nunique projection carrier T_D(x) is given by the following complete rules:\n\n* T_D=0 if c+sum b_j|z_j|<=0;\n* T_D=1 if 1-c-sum b_j(|z_j|-b_j)_+<=0;\n* otherwise T_D is the unique interior root of Psi_x(t)=0.\n\nThe interior root is an explicit finite piecewise formula, not a feasibility\noracle. Order the predetermined query ratios r_j=|z_j|/b_j decreasingly,\nbreaking equal ratios by the coordinate index. Set r_(0)=infinity and\nr_(q+1)=0. For each k=0,...,q form the mathematical expression\n\n    t_k=[c+sum_(j in first k ratios)b_j |z_j|]\n                       /[1+sum_(j in first k ratios)b_j^2].\n\nThe root is t_k for an index with r_(k+1)<=t_k<=r_(k) and 0<t_k<1.\nExistence follows by locating the unique root among these breakpoint cells.\nAt a tied breakpoint several indices may qualify and give the same number;\nthe strictly increasing Psi proves uniqueness of the number. This is an\nexact formula for any finite rank; no enumeration, sorting, root solver or\ntrajectory was executed in this round.\n\nThe complete real projected vector and active face are\n\n    P_D(x)=(T_D(x),w_1(T_D(x)),...,w_q(T_D(x))).\n\nIf T_D=0 the projected face is the origin. If T_D=1 the cap is active.\nEach coordinate with |z_j|>b_j T_D has its sign-specified lateral constraint\nactive; coordinates at equality also lie on that constraint. Unsaturated\ncoordinates lie within the lateral interval. These equalities, and t=0 or\nt=1 when applicable, specify the minimal face. All signs and tie cases are\nincluded. This accounts for every vertex, every lateral plane and the cap,\nincluding planes that become active much later in the run.\n\nFor C the identical formula uses t>=0 without the upper endpoint1. Denote\nits unique nonnegative threshold by T_C and its projected vector by P_C.\nWhen T_C<=1 it is also the D projection, since the minimizer over C then\nbelongs to its subset D.\n\nFor every physical index of any supplied word define, mathematically,\n\n    g_i=P_D(x_i), r_i=x_i-g_i, delta_i=<r_i,g_i>,\n    f_i=F(x_i)=delta_i+||g_i||^2/2,\n    x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i g_i,       0<=i<n.\n\nThe same projection formula defines the actual old terminal g_n and f_n.\nThese are finite exact piecewise expressions fixed by h and the parameters,\nnot a computed transcript or freely selected gradients. Zero steps repeat\nthe identical jet and all original indices remain. The formula supplies\nthe full word-to-face map in every dimension, at every depth; the bounds\nbelow control its full depth uniformly rather than extrapolating from a\nfinite sample.\n\n## 3. Fixed global function, all directed rows, and old true distance\n\nThe strictly concave maximum defining F has a unique maximizer P_D(x).\nIts normal condition is\n\n    <x-P_D(x),v-P_D(x)><=0 for every v in D.\n\nAdding two such conditions with the two projected vectors proves\n<x-y,P_D(x)-P_D(y)> >= ||P_D(x)-P_D(y)||^2. Thus projection is 1-Lipschitz.\nEvaluation at the two maximizing vectors bounds\nF(y)-F(x)-<P_D(x),y-x> between0 and\n<P_D(y)-P_D(x),y-x>. This proves differentiability with gradient P_D,\nconvexity, finiteness and global smoothness at most1. D has interior by its\ndescription, where P_D(x)=x and F(x)=||x||^2/2, so its least smoothness is1.\nConsequently the displayed recurrence is the actual GD on this one function.\nEquivalently F is the unit Moreau envelope of the fully specified support J:\nif r=x-g is a projection normal, g maximizes <r,v> on D, so g is a support\nsubgradient at r and minimizes J(r)+||x-r||^2/2. Its value is delta+||g||^2/2.\n\nThe exact residual of any ordered pair of actual samples is\n\n    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2\n        =<r_j,g_i-g_j><=0.\n\nThe normal condition applies to every g_i in D, including remote samples,\nthe unqueried terminal and any zero gradient. This is an exhaustive proof\nof both directions of every original interpolation row, not merely of their\nadjacent or cyclic sums. Any full gradient/initial Gram is PSD because all\nvectors have the displayed real Euclidean coordinates.\n\nThe complete old minimum set and value are\n\n    min F=0,       argmin F=C^polar.\n\nTo see necessity, if <x,v_s> is positive for a vertex, a sufficiently small\npositive multiple of v_s belongs to D and gives positive objective. If all\nthese inner products are nonpositive, every objective term is nonpositive\nand zero attains the maximum. This proves the full set. Since x_0 belongs\nto C, every minimum y has <x_0,y><=0. Therefore\n\n    dist(x_0,argmin F)^2=||x_0||^2=kappa^2+Z,\n    Z=sum_j z_(0,j)^2,\n\nand zero is the unique closest minimum. No inverse-Gram norm or a selected\nnonclosest minimum replaces this distance.\n\n## 4. Complete fixed prefix completion, with every old anchor retained\n\nRetain every physical old prefix jet at 0<=i<n and the optimum jet at zero.\nFor n>=1 define\n\n    H_pre(v)=||v||^2/2+max(0,max_(0<=i<n){<r_i,v>-delta_i}),\n    F_pre(x)=sup_v{<x,v>-H_pre(v)}.\n\nEach individual jet lower quadratic from accepted1252 reduces exactly to\n||v||^2/2+<r_i,v>-delta_i, since f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2.\nNo first-query, returned-face, remote or zero-step anchor is dropped. All\nslopes r_i, offsets delta_i and weights1 in the maximum are given by the\npreceding explicit projection formula. Thus this is a function determined\nfrom the full known word BEFORE the run. Mathematical recursion defining\nthose finite coefficients is not an executed oracle.\n\nFact_id1252b8823bb5a290 applies because all original prefix and optimum rows\nwere just proved. It gives one everywhere finite convex differentiable\n1-smooth F_pre, its full prefix interpolation and its pointwise maximality\namong legal functions interpolating these jets and the optimum. Induction\non the original physical indices proves that actual GD on the fixed F_pre\nhas every promised old prefix point, value and gradient. Its unqueried\nterminal is the actual F_pre(x_n) with gradient determined by this conjugate,\nnot the old projection gradient unless separately shown. Both orientations\nof all terminal and optimum rows hold by the accepted interpolation\nnecessity applied to this actual function.\n\nSince delta_i>=0 by the old normal comparison with zero, H_pre(0)=0 and\nF_pre>=0. Its complete minimum set is\n\n    argmin F_pre=conv({0} union {r_i:0<=i<n, delta_i=0}).     (A)\n\nIndeed F_pre(x)=0 precisely when zero maximizes its defining conjugate,\nthat is, x belongs to the subgradient of H_pre at zero. Exactly the displayed\nzero-offset affine terms are active there. This proves the full set and\nattainment, including all old zero-height anchors.\n\nFor n=0 use H_pre(v)=||v||^2/2 and F_pre=||x||^2/2. Its minimum is zero\nand the empty word is kept. The diagnostic below has n>0.\n\n## 5. SAME enclosing-m diagnostic and all-depth prefix products\n\nFix m>=1536 and tau=1/4. All subwords retain these SAME enclosing parameters:\n\n    lambda=1+sqrt(2), e=1/(4m), beta=1-e,\n    c=lambda(1+e), a=beta/c,\n    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a c^j,H_j), 0<=j<m,\n    h=H_m, n=2^m-1, S=sum_(i<n)h_i.\n\nEvery physical step is retained. Its excess u_i=h_i-1 is a c^(nu_2(i+1));\nnu_2 is the exponent of2 dividing a positive integer. Let\n\n    A_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, pi_i=product_(t<i)(1-h_t), p_i=|pi_i|,\n    pi_0=p_0=1,\n    P=product_(i<n)u_i=beta^n c^(-m), Qunit=P^2.\n\nNo product or recurrence was executed. Here is the complete symbolic count.\nFor the fixed enclosing parameters the full product on H_j, denoted P_j,\nobeys P_0=1, P_(j+1)=P_j^2 a c^j. Induction gives\nP_j=beta^(2^j-1)c^(-j), including j=0. In particular P_m=P.\n\nEvery nonempty prefix of H_j has product at most a, and every prefix\nincluding the empty one has product at most1. Prove this simultaneously\nby induction. H_1 has its nonempty product a. In H_(j+1), j>=1, prefixes\nwithin the left child have the claimed bound. The product through its\nmiddle bridge is\n\n    P_j a c^j=beta^(2^j)/c<=a.\n\nMultiplying this by any prefix product in the right child, bounded by1,\nproves the assertion on all remaining prefixes. This accounts for every\ninherited physical state, without resetting m or child parameters. Thus\n\n    p_i<=a for every1<=i<=n,       u_i>=a>0.              (B)\n\nFor transparent constants, e<=1/64 and lambda<29/12 imply\n2/5<a<1/2, 2<c<5/2. For the lower bound,\na>(12/29)(63/65)=756/1885>2/5; the upper follows from beta<1 and c>2.\nThe level1 bridge is k_bridge=1+beta<2; leaves are l=1+a<3/2.\n\nWriting v=a/(c-2), the exact full physical mass is\n\n    S=v c^m+(1-v)2^m-1,       4/5<v<1.                 (C)\n\nThe equality follows by summing the counts2^(m-j-1) at each levelj.\nThe lower bound on v follows from a>2/5 and c-2<1/2. For v<1 use\nc(c-2)>lambda(lambda-2)=1>beta. Since c>12/5 and m>=5,\nc^m>51, and (C) gives\n\n    c^m/2<S<c^m,       S>5,       Qunit<=c^(-2m).       (D)\n\nThe comparison (12/5)^5>51 is the integer inequality248832>159375.\n\n## 6. Two cone inequalities proved from normals\n\nFor x=g+r with g=P_C(x), the normal comparisons with zero and2g show\n\n    r in C^polar, <r,g>=0,       F_C(x)=||g||^2/2,\n\nwhere F_C is the same quadratic conjugate restricted to the unbounded cone.\nThe normal proof of projection nonexpansiveness applies also to C, and\nP_C(r)=0. For a physical step h>1, put u=h-1. Then\n\n    ||P_C(x-hg)||=||P_C(r-u g)-P_C(r)||<=u||g||.        (E)\n\nThere is a second, coordinate-specific inequality needed to pay the CAP,\nwhich a norm bound alone does not establish in growing dimension. Write\ng=(t,w), with |w_j|<=b_j t. At t'=u t the threshold function for x'=r-u g\nsatisfies\n\n    Psi_(x')(u t)\n      =2u t-r_0-sum_j b_j(|r_j-u w_j|-b_j u t)_+\n      >=2u t-r_0-sum_j b_j|r_j|>=0.\n\nThe first inequality uses |w_j|<=b_j t, and the second is the full polar\ncondition r_0+sum b_j|r_j|<=0. Strict monotonicity of Psi gives\n\n    T_C(x-hg)<=u t.                                    (F)\n\nBoth inequalities retain the entire normal vector r. No old transverse\nstate is cancelled or discarded. For 0<=h<=1, the normal pair instead\ngives P_C(x-hg)=(1-h)g; the original diagnostic uses only h>1.\n\n## 7. Hence its cone carrier is at mostT.\nIf u>=1, T=p_(k+1)<=a. If u<1, T=p_k<=a.
